# A/B checker results

Generated by `src/finetune/checker_table.py`. Plan: `docs/CHECKER.md`.

## 1. Does the checker pick what is written? (held-out pairs)

Accuracy = share of pairs where the right option gets more than half the probability, averaged over both option orders. Chance is 50%. The fix only pays off if *EDU misread* is well above 50% while *EDU correct number* stays near 100%.

| Pairs | base | reader | checker_f | checker_fe |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| EDU misread: picks the true value | 53.1% (32% both orders, n=196) | 51.5% (30% both orders, n=196) | 46.9% (36% both orders, n=196) | 57.1% (49% both orders, n=196) |
| EDU correct number: keeps it | 90.2% (76% both orders, n=400) | 95.0% (82% both orders, n=400) | 99.5% (100% both orders, n=400) | 99.5% (99% both orders, n=400) |
| FERMAT wrong value: keeps the written one | 80.8% (63% both orders, n=78) | 82.0% (67% both orders, n=78) | 94.9% (94% both orders, n=78) | 92.3% (91% both orders, n=78) |
| FERMAT correct number: keeps it | 93.5% (83% both orders, n=400) | 93.0% (80% both orders, n=400) | 99.2% (98% both orders, n=400) | 98.2% (98% both orders, n=400) |


## 2. Automatic correction with the checker (unseen students; `_k10_beams` = 10 numbers per page with beam candidates, otherwise the 3 most doubtful)

Success = more *Fixed* than *Correct numbers broken* + *Changed to another wrong value*, and fewer wrong numbers per exam after than before.

| Run | Numbers checked | of them misreads | True value among options | …among beams | Fixed | Changed to another wrong value | Correct numbers broken | Misreads before → after | Wrong numbers per exam before → after |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| checker_fe | 569 | 106 | 34% | – | **5** | 0 | **3** | 304 → 302 | 12.958 → 12.951 |
| checker_fe_k10_beams | 1826 | 206 | 69% | 69% | **10** | 2 | **7** | 304 → 301 | 12.958 → 12.951 |
| reader | 569 | 106 | 34% | – | **2** | 1 | **4** | 304 → 306 | 12.958 → 12.965 |


## 4. Learned Arbiter: all tool evidence combined (unseen students)

Checker probability and margin, order agreement, blank-image probability, beam rank and probability, reader doubt, edit shape and answer-key flags in one logistic model, trained on other students. *net*: threshold with the most net fixes on the training students (no change unless positive); *budget*: at most 1% of correct numbers changed.

| Run | Rule | Fixable (true value offered) | Fixed | Changed to another wrong value | Correct broken | Net | Misreads before → after | Arbiter AUROC | Checker alone AUROC |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| checker_fe | net | 28 | **0** | 0 | 0 | **+0** | 304 → 304 | 0.8475 | 0.8604 |
| checker_fe | budget | 28 | **4** | 1 | 4 | **-1** | 304 → 304 | 0.8475 | 0.8604 |
| checker_fe_k10_beams | net | 97 | **6** | 3 | 12 | **-9** | 304 → 310 | 0.8681 | 0.8342 |
| checker_fe_k10_beams | budget | 97 | **19** | 4 | 17 | **-2** | 304 → 302 | 0.8681 | 0.8342 |
| reader | net | 29 | **0** | 0 | 0 | **+0** | 304 → 304 | 0.8876 | 0.8108 |
| reader | budget | 29 | **3** | 0 | 3 | **+0** | 304 → 304 | 0.8876 | 0.8108 |

# A/B checker results

Generated by `src/finetune/checker_table.py`. Plan: `docs/CHECKER.md`.

## 1. Does the checker pick what is written? (held-out pairs)

Accuracy = share of pairs where the right option gets more than half the probability, averaged over both option orders. Chance is 50%. The fix only pays off if *EDU misread* is well above 50% while *EDU correct number* stays near 100%.

| Pairs | base | reader | checker_f | checker_fe |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| EDU misread: picks the true value | 53.1% (32% both orders, n=196) | 51.5% (30% both orders, n=196) | 46.9% (36% both orders, n=196) | 57.1% (49% both orders, n=196) |
| EDU correct number: keeps it | 90.2% (76% both orders, n=400) | 95.0% (82% both orders, n=400) | 99.5% (100% both orders, n=400) | 99.5% (99% both orders, n=400) |
| FERMAT wrong value: keeps the written one | 80.8% (63% both orders, n=78) | 82.0% (67% both orders, n=78) | 94.9% (94% both orders, n=78) | 92.3% (91% both orders, n=78) |
| FERMAT correct number: keeps it | 93.5% (83% both orders, n=400) | 93.0% (80% both orders, n=400) | 99.2% (98% both orders, n=400) | 98.2% (98% both orders, n=400) |


## 2. Automatic correction with the checker (unseen students; `_k10_beams` = 10 numbers per page with beam candidates, otherwise the 3 most doubtful)

Success = more *Fixed* than *Correct numbers broken* + *Changed to another wrong value*, and fewer wrong numbers per exam after than before.

| Run | Numbers checked | of them misreads | True value among options | …among beams | Fixed | Changed to another wrong value | Correct numbers broken | Misreads before → after | Wrong numbers per exam before → after |

| checker_fe_k10_beams | 1826 | 206 | 69% | 69% | **10** | 2 | **7** | 304 → 301 | 12.958 → 12.951 |
| reader | 569 | 106 | 34% | – | **2** | 1 | **4** | 304 → 306 | 12.958 → 12.965 |

## 4. Learned Arbiter: all tool evidence combined (unseen students)

Checker probability and margin, order agreement, blank-image probability, beam rank and probability, reader doubt, edit shape and answer-key flags in one logistic model, trained on other students. *net*: threshold with the most net fixes on the training students (no change unless positive); *budget*: at most 1% of correct numbers changed.

| checker_fe | budget | 28 | **4** | 1 | 4 | **-1** | 304 → 304 | 0.8475 | 0.8604 |
| checker_fe_k10_beams | net | 97 | **6** | 3 | 12 | **-9** | 304 → 310 | 0.8681 | 0.8342 |

# A/B checker results

Generated by `src/finetune/checker_table.py`. Plan: `docs/CHECKER.md`.

## 1. Does the checker pick what is written? (held-out pairs)

Accuracy = share of pairs where the right option gets more than half the probability, averaged over both option orders. Chance is 50%. The fix only pays off if *EDU misread* is well above 50% while *EDU correct number* stays near 100%.

| Pairs | base | reader | checker_f | checker_fe |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| EDU misread: picks the true value | 53.1% (32% both orders, n=196) | 51.5% (30% both orders, n=196) | 46.9% (36% both orders, n=196) | 57.1% (49% both orders, n=196) |
| EDU correct number: keeps it | 90.2% (76% both orders, n=400) | 95.0% (82% both orders, n=400) | 99.5% (100% both orders, n=400) | 99.5% (99% both orders, n=400) |
| FERMAT wrong value: keeps the written one | 80.8% (63% both orders, n=78) | 82.0% (67% both orders, n=78) | 94.9% (94% both orders, n=78) | 92.3% (91% both orders, n=78) |
| FERMAT correct number: keeps it | 93.5% (83% both orders, n=400) | 93.0% (80% both orders, n=400) | 99.2% (98% both orders, n=400) | 98.2% (98% both orders, n=400) |


## 2. Automatic correction with the checker (unseen students; `_k10_beams` = 10 numbers per page with beam candidates, otherwise the 3 most doubtful)

Success = more *Fixed* than *Correct numbers broken* + *Changed to another wrong value*, and fewer wrong numbers per exam after than before.

| Run | Numbers checked | of them misreads | True value among options | …among beams | Fixed | Changed to another wrong value | Correct numbers broken | Misreads before → after | Wrong numbers per exam before → after |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| checker_fe | 569 | 106 | 34% | – | **5** | 0 | **3** | 304 → 302 | 12.958 → 12.951 |
| checker_fe_k10_beams | 1826 | 206 | 69% | 69% | **10** | 2 | **7** | 304 → 301 | 12.958 → 12.951 |
| reader | 569 | 106 | 34% | – | **2** | 1 | **4** | 304 → 306 | 12.958 → 12.965 |


## 3. FERMAT pairs without the red error markers

| Pairs | base | reader | checker_f | checker_fe |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| FERMAT wrong value: keeps the written one | 82.2% (66% both orders, n=382) | 86.9% (70% both orders, n=382) | 97.9% (97% both orders, n=382) | 94.5% (93% both orders, n=382) |
| FERMAT correct number: keeps it | 94.0% (85% both orders, n=400) | 96.0% (83% both orders, n=400) | 100.0% (100% both orders, n=400) | 99.0% (99% both orders, n=400) |


## 4. Learned Arbiter: all tool evidence combined (unseen students)

Checker probability and margin, order agreement, blank-image probability, beam rank and probability, reader doubt, edit shape and answer-key flags in one logistic model, trained on other students. *net*: threshold with the most net fixes on the training students (no change unless positive); *budget*: at most 1% of correct numbers changed.

| Run | Rule | Fixable (true value offered) | Fixed | Changed to another wrong value | Correct broken | Net | Misreads before → after | Arbiter AUROC | Checker alone AUROC |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| checker_fe | net | 28 | **0** | 0 | 0 | **+0** | 304 → 304 | 0.8475 | 0.8604 |
| checker_fe | budget | 28 | **4** | 1 | 4 | **-1** | 304 → 304 | 0.8475 | 0.8604 |
| checker_fe_k10_beams | net | 97 | **6** | 3 | 12 | **-9** | 304 → 310 | 0.8681 | 0.8342 |
| checker_fe_k10_beams | budget | 97 | **19** | 4 | 17 | **-2** | 304 → 302 | 0.8681 | 0.8342 |
| reader | net | 29 | **0** | 0 | 0 | **+0** | 304 → 304 | 0.8876 | 0.8108 |
| reader | budget | 29 | **3** | 0 | 3 | **+0** | 304 → 304 | 0.8876 | 0.8108 |
